Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a galactic algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 599. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 370/2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to galactic structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 599/370 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The galactic operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 933. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 796 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 933 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the galactic structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 796. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
